<p id="mwCw">The <b id="mwDA">Dassam Falls</b> (also known as <b id="mwDQ">Dassam Ghagh</b>) is a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Waterfall" title="Waterfall" id="mwDg">waterfall</a> located near Taimara village in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bundu,_India" title="Bundu, India" id="mwDw">Bundu</a> subdivision of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ranchi_district" title="Ranchi district" id="mwEA">Ranchi district</a> in the Indian <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/States_and_territories_of_India" title="States and territories of India" class="mw-redirect" id="mwEQ">state</a> of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jharkhand" title="Jharkhand" id="mwEg">Jharkhand</a>.<sup about="#mwt13" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-1" id="mwEw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwFA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFQ">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFg">]</span></span></a></sup> The Falls are 144 feet in height, formed by the waterflow of the Kanchi River.<sup about="#mwt16" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-2"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2" id="mwFw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwGA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGQ">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" id="mwGw" data-mw='{"autoGenerated":true}'/></section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwHA" aria-labelledby="Etymology"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Etymology">Etymology</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Dassam_Falls&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwHQ">Dassam is a changed form of word Da:song which means in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mundari_language" title="Mundari language" id="mwHg">mundari language</a> the act of pouring water. Da: means water and song means pouring or measuring. The water fall resembles like somebody is pouring water so the name was Da:song earlier but afterwards the name was changed to Dassam.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwKA" aria-labelledby="Location"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Location">Location</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Dassam_Falls&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Location"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwKQ">Dassam Falls is located at <span class="geo-inline" about="#mwt20" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwKg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"coord","href":"./Template:Coord"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"23.143358"},"2":{"wt":"N"},"3":{"wt":"85.466441"},"4":{"wt":"E "},"5":{"wt":"source:yahoomaps_region:IN "},"display":{"wt":"inline"}},"i":0}}]}'><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1156832818" about="#mwt21" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Coordinates/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'/><span class="plainlinks nourlexpansion"><a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://geohack.toolforge.org/geohack.php?pagename=Dassam_Falls&amp;params=23.143358_N_85.466441_E_source:yahoomaps_region:IN" class="external text"><span class="geo-nondefault"><span class="geo-dms" title="Maps, aerial photos, and other data for this location"><span class="latitude">23°08′36″N</span> <span class="longitude">85°27′59″E</span></span></span><span class="geo-multi-punct"><span typeof="mw:Entity">﻿</span> / <span typeof="mw:Entity">﻿</span></span><span class="geo-default"><span class="geo-dec" title="Maps, aerial photos, and other data for this location">23.143358°N 85.466441°E</span><span style="display:none"><span typeof="mw:Entity">﻿</span> / <span class="geo">23.143358; 85.466441</span></span></span></a></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Pages_using_gadget_WikiMiniAtlas"/></span>.</p>

<p id="mwKw"><small id="mwLA">Note: The map alongside presents some of the notable locations in the district. All places marked in the map are linked in the larger full screen map.</small></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="4" id="mwLQ" aria-labelledby="The_falls"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="The_falls">The falls</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Dassam_Falls&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: The falls"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwLg"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Dasam_fall.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwLw"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Dasam_fall.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a7/Dasam_fall.jpg/250px-Dasam_fall.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a7/Dasam_fall.jpg/500px-Dasam_fall.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="4608" data-file-height="3456" data-file-type="bitmap" height="188" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwMA"/></a><figcaption id="mwMQ">Dasam Falls</figcaption></figure>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwMg"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Dassam_Falls,_Jharkhand,_India_04.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwMw"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Dassam_Falls,_Jharkhand,_India_04.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/f0/Dassam_Falls%2C_Jharkhand%2C_India_04.jpg/250px-Dassam_Falls%2C_Jharkhand%2C_India_04.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/f0/Dassam_Falls%2C_Jharkhand%2C_India_04.jpg/500px-Dassam_Falls%2C_Jharkhand%2C_India_04.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" alt="Tourists at Dassam Falls" data-file-width="4000" data-file-height="3000" data-file-type="bitmap" height="188" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwNA"/></a><figcaption id="mwNQ">Tourists at Dassam Falls</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwNg">The Dassam Falls is a natural cascade across the Kanchi River, a tributary of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Subarnarekha_River" title="Subarnarekha River" id="mwNw">Subarnarekha River</a>. The water falls from a height of  <span about="#mwt22" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwOA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"44"},"2":{"wt":"m"}},"i":0}}]}'>44 metres (144</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt22"> </span><span about="#mwt22">ft)</span>.<sup about="#mwt25" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-3"}}'><a href="#cite_note-3" id="mwOQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwOg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwOw">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPA">]</span></span></a></sup> The sound of water echoes all around the place.<sup about="#mwt28" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-maps_4-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"maps"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-maps-4"}}'><a href="#cite_note-maps-4" id="mwPQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwPg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQA">]</span></span></a></sup> Dassam Falls at one of the edges of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chota_Nagpur_Plateau#Ranchi_plateau" title="Chota Nagpur Plateau" id="mwQQ">Ranchi plateau</a> is one of the many <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fault_scarp" title="Fault scarp" id="mwQg">scarp</a> falls in the region.<sup about="#mwt31" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-5" id="mwQw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwRA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRQ">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwRw">The Dassam Falls is an example of a nick point caused by rejuvenation. <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Knickpoint" title="Knickpoint" id="mwSA">Knick point</a>, also called a nick point or simply nick, represents breaks in slopes in the longitudinal profile of a river caused by rejuvenation. The break in channel gradient allows water to fall vertically giving rise to a waterfall.<sup about="#mwt36" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-6" id="mwSQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwSg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSw">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwTQ">The water of the Dassam Falls is very clean and clear. It is natural for a tourist to be enticed  to enter the water for a bath or swim but tourists are warned not to do so because of the current that is generated.  There have been many cases of drowning in Dassam Falls.<sup about="#mwt39" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-7" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-7" id="mwTg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwTw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUA">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUQ">]</span></span></a></sup> Nine people died of drowning between 2001 and 2006. 
<sup about="#mwt42" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-8" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-8"}}'><a href="#cite_note-8" id="mwUg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwUw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVA">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="5" id="mwVg" aria-labelledby="Transport"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Transport">Transport</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Dassam_Falls&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Transport"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwVw">The Dassam Falls is <span about="#mwt43" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwWA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"40"},"2":{"wt":"km"}},"i":0}}]}'>40 kilometres (25</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt43"> </span><span about="#mwt43">mi)</span> from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ranchi" title="Ranchi" id="mwWQ">Ranchi</a> on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Highway_33_(India)" title="National Highway 33 (India)" id="mwWg">NH 33</a> (now <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Highway_18_(India)" title="National Highway 18 (India)" id="mwWw">NH-18</a>) or Ranchi-Jamshedpur highway.</p>
